logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.tools.registry")

TOOL_INPUT_MODELS: Dict[str, Any] = {
    "run_jadx": RunJadxInput,
    "inspect_elf_symbols": InspectElfSymbolsInput,
    "search_strings": SearchStringsInput,
    "find_payload_candidates": FindPayloadCandidatesInput,
    "find_xrefs": FindXrefsInput,
    "repair_container": RepairContainerInput,
    "inspect_container": InspectContainerInput,
    "decode_static_payload": DecodeStaticPayloadInput,
    "discover_capabilities": DiscoverCapabilitiesInput,
    "discover_tools": DiscoverToolsInput,
    "get_tool_schema": GetToolSchemaInput,
    "inspect_state": InspectStateInput,
    "python_exec": PythonExecInput,
    "shell_exec": ShellExecInput,
    "read_file": ReadFileInput,
    "write_file": WriteFileInput,
    "create_derived_artifact": CreateDerivedArtifactInput,
    "list_directory": ListDirectoryInput,
    "inspect_artifact": InspectArtifactInput,
    "apply_patch": ApplyPatchInput,
    "run_scoped_pytest": RunScopedPytestInput,
    "git_diff": GitDiffInput,
    "git_checkpoint": GitCheckpointInput,
    "rollback": RollbackInput,
    "verify_repair_session": VerifyRepairSessionInput,
}


class ToolRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self._tools: Dict[str, BaseTool] = {}
        self._type_adapter = TypeAdapter(ToolCallInput)

    def register(self, tool: BaseTool) -> None:
        self._tools[tool.name] = tool
        logger.info(f"Registered tool: {tool.name} (v{tool.version}, category: {tool.category})")

    def register_tool(self, tool: BaseTool) -> None:
        self.register(tool)

    def get_tool(self, name: str) -> Optional[BaseTool]:
        return self._tools.get(name)

    def list_tools(self) -> List[str]:
        """Return list of all registered tool names."""
        return list(self._tools.keys())

    def _get_schema_for_tool(self, tool: BaseTool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        input_model = TOOL_INPUT_MODELS.get(tool.name)
        if input_model:
            model_schema = input_model.model_json_schema()
            properties = {k: v for k, v in model_schema.get("properties", {}).items() if k != "tool"}
            required = [r for r in model_schema.get("required", []) if r != "tool"]
            parameters = {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": properties,
                "required": required,
            }
        else:
            parameters = {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "artifact_id": {"type": "string", "description": "Target file or artifact path"},
                },
            }
        return {
            "type": "function",
            "function": {
                "name": tool.name,
                "description": tool.description,
                "parameters": parameters,
            },
        }

    def get_tools_schema(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Returns OpenAI-compatible function calling schemas for all registered tools."""
        return [self._get_schema_for_tool(tool) for tool in self._tools.values()]

    def discover_capabilities(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
        grouped: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
        for tool in self._tools.values():
            cat = tool.family.value.lower()
            if cat not in grouped:
                grouped[cat] = []
            grouped[cat].append(tool.name)
        if "reconstruction" in grouped and "repair_container" not in grouped["reconstruction"]:
            grouped["reconstruction"].append("repair_container")
        return grouped

    def list_capabilities(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
        """Alias for discover_capabilities for contract compatibility."""
        return self.discover_capabilities()

    def discover_tools(self, family: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        fam_norm = family.strip().lower()
        tools = []
        for tool in self._tools.values():
            t_fam = tool.family.value.lower()
            if t_fam == fam_norm or (fam_norm == "reconstruction" and tool.name == "repair_container"):
                tools.append({
                    "name": tool.name,
                    "description": tool.description,
                })
        return tools

    def get_tool_schema(self, tool_name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        tool = self.get_tool(tool_name)
        if not tool:
            return None
        return self._get_schema_for_tool(tool)

    def validate_input(self, raw_input: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolCallInput:
        """Validates raw dictionary against discriminated union ToolCallInput."""
        return self._type_adapter.validate_python(raw_input)

    async def execute_tool(self, raw_input: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
        """Validate tool input, then execute the registered tool.

        Fail-closed: input that does not validate against the tool's schema is rejected
        and the tool's execute() is never entered.
        """
        tool_name = raw_input.get("tool") if isinstance(raw_input, dict) else None

        try:
            validated_input: ToolCallInput = self.validate_input(raw_input)
        except ValidationError as ve:
            logger.warning(f"Rejected tool call '{tool_name}': input validation failed ({ve.error_count()} errors)")
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=str(tool_name or "unknown"),
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=0.0,
                output_summary=f"Input validation failed for tool '{tool_name}'; the tool was not executed: {ve}",
                error="INPUT_VALIDATION_FAILED",
            )

        tool = self.get_tool(validated_input.tool)
        if not tool:
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=validated_input.tool,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=0.0,
                output_summary=f"Tool '{validated_input.tool}' is not registered in ToolRegistry",
                error="TOOL_NOT_FOUND",
            )

        if not await tool.is_available():
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=tool.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.SKIPPED,
                duration_seconds=0.0,
                output_summary=f"Tool '{tool.name}' is currently unavailable in the environment",
                error="TOOL_UNAVAILABLE",
            )

        return await tool.execute(validated_input, context)


# Self-repair tools mutate the KAVACH source tree (patch, test, commit, rollback).
# They are registered ONLY in the repair registry; the investigation registry must never
# contain them. Registration is the authorization boundary, not prompt instructions.
REPAIR_TOOL_NAMES = frozenset({
    "apply_patch",
    "run_scoped_pytest",
    "git_diff",
    "git_checkpoint",
    "rollback",
    "verify_repair_session",
})


def build_investigation_tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
    """Registry for forensic investigations: inspection, discovery, reconstruction, sandboxed workbench.

    Execution plane: every external process launched by these tools goes through the ExecutionBroker
    (run_jadx and inspect_elf_symbols via tools/brokered.py; python_exec/shell_exec via workbench/tools.py).
    The remaining tools (inspect_container, repair_container, search_strings, find_xrefs,
    find_payload_candidates, decode_static_payload, meta and file tools) are in-process Python parsers
    with no external executable, so there is no process to broker; they parse hostile bytes in the
    KAVACH process, which the ArtifactStore/execution phases may move out of process. Quark, Androguard,
    APKiD and YARA-X run only in the deterministic orchestrator and are not agent-reachable.
    test_forensic_execution_broker.py statically forbids direct process APIs under tools/.
    """
    from tools.forensic.jadx_tool import JadxTool
    from tools.forensic.elf_native_tool import ElfNativeTool
    from tools.forensic.inspect_container_tool import InspectContainerTool
    from tools.discovery.search_strings_tool import SearchStringsTool
    from tools.discovery.find_xrefs_tool import FindXrefsTool
    from tools.discovery.find_payload_candidates_tool import FindPayloadCandidatesTool
    from tools.reconstruction.container_repair_tool import ContainerRepairTool
    from tools.reconstruction.payload_decoder_tool import PayloadDecoderTool
    from tools.meta.registry_tools import DiscoverCapabilitiesTool, DiscoverToolsTool, GetToolSchemaTool
    from tools.meta.inspect_state_tool import InspectStateTool
    from workbench.tools import (
        PythonExecTool,
        ShellExecTool,
        ReadFileTool,
        WriteFileTool,
        CreateDerivedArtifactTool,
        ListDirectoryTool,
        InspectArtifactTool,
    )

    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register(InspectContainerTool())
    registry.register(ContainerRepairTool())
    registry.register(JadxTool())
    registry.register(ElfNativeTool())
    registry.register(SearchStringsTool())
    registry.register(FindXrefsTool())
    registry.register(FindPayloadCandidatesTool())
    registry.register(PayloadDecoderTool())

    registry.register(DiscoverCapabilitiesTool(registry))
    registry.register(DiscoverToolsTool(registry))
    registry.register(GetToolSchemaTool(registry))
    registry.register(InspectStateTool())

    # Workbench Tools
    registry.register(PythonExecTool())
    registry.register(ShellExecTool())
    registry.register(ReadFileTool())
    registry.register(WriteFileTool())
    registry.register(CreateDerivedArtifactTool())
    registry.register(ListDirectoryTool())
    registry.register(InspectArtifactTool())

    leaked = REPAIR_TOOL_NAMES & set(registry.list_tools())
    if leaked:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Repair tools must not be registered for investigations: {sorted(leaked)}")
    return registry


def build_repair_tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
    """Registry for the developer self-repair workflow (git worktree patch/test/checkpoint)."""
    from workbench.tools import (
        ApplyPatchTool,
        RunScopedPytestTool,
        GitDiffTool,
        GitCheckpointTool,
        RollbackTool,
        VerifyRepairSessionTool,
    )

    registry = ToolRegistry()
    registry.register(ApplyPatchTool())
    registry.register(RunScopedPytestTool())
    registry.register(GitDiffTool())
    registry.register(VerifyRepairSessionTool())
    registry.register(GitCheckpointTool())
    registry.register(RollbackTool())
    return registry


# Global investigation registry instance (never contains repair tools)
global_tool_registry = build_investigation_tool_registry()

# ============================================================================
# Helper Context Resolver
# ============================================================================

def _get_or_create_resolver(context: Dict[str, Any]) -> PathResolver:
    """Retrieve PathResolver from context or construct from standard paths."""
    if "path_resolver" in context and isinstance(context["path_resolver"], PathResolver):
        resolver = context["path_resolver"]
        # Keep artifact handles registered after resolver creation (e.g. derived artifacts) resolvable.
        for art_id, art_path in context.get("known_artifacts", {}).items():
            if resolver.get_artifact_path(art_id) is None and os.path.exists(art_path):
                resolver.register_artifact(art_id, art_path)
        return resolver

    work_dir = context.get("work_dir") or "/tmp/kavach_forensic_workspace"
    apk_path = context.get("apk_path")
    if apk_path and os.path.exists(apk_path):
        input_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(apk_path))
    else:
        input_root = "/tmp/kavach_input_root"

    forensic_root = os.path.join(work_dir, "forensic")
    repair_root = os.path.join(work_dir, "repair")
    artifact_root = os.path.join(work_dir, "artifacts")

    resolver = PathResolver(
        input_root=input_root,
        forensic_root=forensic_root,
        repair_root=repair_root,
        artifact_root=artifact_root,
        allow_repair_workspace=False,
    )

    # Register known artifacts if provided in context
    known_artifacts = context.get("known_artifacts", {})
    for art_id, art_path in known_artifacts.items():
        if os.path.exists(art_path):
            resolver.register_artifact(art_id, art_path)

    context["path_resolver"] = resolver
    return resolver


def _get_or_create_executor(context: Dict[str, Any], resolver: PathResolver) -> WorkbenchExecutor:
    """Retrieve WorkbenchExecutor from context or construct with standard policy."""
    if "workbench_executor" in context and isinstance(context["workbench_executor"], WorkbenchExecutor):
        return context["workbench_executor"]

    policy = context.get("workbench_policy") or WorkbenchPolicy()
    executor = WorkbenchExecutor(path_resolver=resolver, policy=policy)
    context["workbench_executor"] = executor
    return executor


def _get_or_create_execution_broker(context: Dict[str, Any], resolver: PathResolver) -> ExecutionBroker:
    """Retrieve ExecutionBroker from context or construct wrapping WorkbenchExecutor via BwrapBackend."""

        except Exception as e:
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=0.01,
                output_summary=f"Write file failed for '{tool_input.uri}': {e}",
                error=str(e),
                warnings=[str(e)],
            )


class CreateDerivedArtifactTool(BaseTool):
    name = "create_derived_artifact"
    version = "1.0.0"
    category = "filesystem"
    family = ToolFamily.WORKBENCH
    description = (
        "Register a derived forensic file from FORENSIC_WORKSPACE as an official ArtifactStore artifact. "
        "Computes SHA-256, size, mime type, and surfaces the artifact handle for subsequent analysis."
    )

    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return True

    async def execute(self, tool_input: ToolCallInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
        resolver = _get_or_create_resolver(context)
        assert isinstance(tool_input, CreateDerivedArtifactInput)

        try:
            raw_bytes = resolver.read_bytes(tool_input.source_uri)
            stat_res = resolver.stat_secure(tool_input.source_uri)
            resolved_path = resolver.resolve_path(tool_input.source_uri, for_write=False)
            sha256 = hashlib.sha256(raw_bytes).hexdigest()
            size = len(raw_bytes)
            mime, _ = mimetypes.guess_type(resolved_path.name)

            art_type_str = tool_input.artifact_type.upper()
            art_type = getattr(ArtifactType, art_type_str, ArtifactType.DEX_FILES)

            art_id = f"art_{resolved_path.stem}_{sha256[:8]}"
            resolver.register_artifact(art_id, resolved_path)

            produced_art = ProducedArtifact(
                id=art_id,
                artifact_type=art_type,
                storage_uri=str(resolved_path),
                sha256=sha256,
                size_bytes=size,
                mime_type=mime or "application/octet-stream",
                producer=self.name,
                producer_version=self.version,
                metadata={
                    "source_uri": tool_input.source_uri,
                    "parent_artifact_id": tool_input.parent_artifact_id,
                    "description": tool_input.description,
                },
            )

            # Register in context known_artifacts
            if "known_artifacts" in context and isinstance(context["known_artifacts"], dict):
                context["known_artifacts"][art_id] = str(resolved_path)

            summary = (
                f"Registered derived artifact: artifact://{art_id}\n"
                f"- Source: {tool_input.source_uri}\n"
                f"- Type: {art_type.value}\n"
                f"- Size: {size} bytes\n"
                f"- SHA-256: {sha256}\n"
                f"- Description: {tool_input.description or '(none)'}"
            )

            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS,
                duration_seconds=0.02,
                output_summary=summary,
                produced_artifacts=[produced_art],
                raw_data={"artifact_id": art_id, "sha256": sha256, "size_bytes": size},
            )
"""
Immutable Result and Context Types for KAVACH AI 2.0 Analyzers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from domain.evidence import Evidence
from domain.execution_state import ExecutionState
from analyzers.capabilities import ArtifactType


class ProducedArtifact(BaseModel):
    """Artifact generated by a preparation step or analyzer."""
    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    artifact_type: ArtifactType
    storage_uri: str
    sha256: Optional[str] = None
    size_bytes: int = 0
    mime_type: Optional[str] = None
    producer: str
    producer_version: str
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class AnalyzerResult(BaseModel):
    """
    Immutable result emitted by an analyzer plugin.
    Returned to orchestrator / persistence service without touching DB sessions directly.
    """
    analyzer_name: str
    analyzer_version: str
    status: ExecutionState
    evidence: List[Evidence] = Field(default_factory=list)
    artifacts: List[ProducedArtifact] = Field(default_factory=list)
    raw_output: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    stdout: Optional[str] = None
    stderr: Optional[str] = None
    exit_code: Optional[int] = 0
    duration_seconds: float = 0.0
    execution_metadata: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    warnings: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class AnalysisContext(BaseModel):
    """
    Context passed to an analyzer or preparation step.
    Contains target APK path, run identity, available artifacts, and temp working directory.
    """
    run_id: str
    sample_id: str
    apk_path: str
    package_name: Optional[str] = None
    work_dir: str
    available_artifacts: Dict[ArtifactType, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    config: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

"""
KAVACH AI 2.0 - JADX Forensic Decompiler Tool.
Executes JADX 1.5.0+ to decompile APKs, DEX files, and smali bytecode.
Supports class filtering, deobfuscation, and structured artifact output.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import os
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, List

from tools.base import BaseTool, ToolFamily, ToolExecutionMode, RunJadxInput, ToolOutput, ToolExecutionStatus
from tools.base import ArtifactHandleError, artifact_handle_rejection, resolve_artifact_handle
from analyzers.results import ProducedArtifact, ArtifactType
from system.toolchain import try_resolve_tool
from execution.models import ExecutionStatus
from tools.brokered import execution_provenance, run_brokered, sandbox_path, stage_artifact


class JadxTool(BaseTool):
    name: str = "run_jadx"
    family = ToolFamily.BYTECODE
    version: str = "1.5.0"
    category: str = "bytecode"
    description: str = "Decompiles APK or DEX bytecode into Java source code with deobfuscation support."
    execution_mode = ToolExecutionMode.EXTERNAL_PROCESS

    def __init__(self):
        self._binary_path: str = self._resolve_binary()

    def _resolve_binary(self) -> str:
        return try_resolve_tool("jadx") or "jadx"

    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return os.path.isfile(self._binary_path) and os.access(self._binary_path, os.X_OK)

    async def execute(self, tool_input: RunJadxInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
        start_time = time.time()
        try:
            artifact_path = resolve_artifact_handle(context, tool_input.artifact_id)
        except ArtifactHandleError as e:
            return artifact_handle_rejection(self.name, start_time, e)

        if not os.path.exists(artifact_path):
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                output_summary=f"Artifact not found on disk: {tool_input.artifact_id}",
                error="ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND",
            )

        try:
            staged = stage_artifact(context, tool_input.artifact_id, artifact_path)
            out_rel = f"jadx/{Path(staged).stem}_{int(time.time())}"
            argv = ["jadx", "-d", out_rel, "--no-res", "-j", "4"]
            if tool_input.deobfuscate:
                argv.append("--deobf")
            argv.append(staged)

            result = await run_brokered(context, "jadx", self.version, argv, tool_input.artifact_id, timeout_seconds=45.0)
            provenance = execution_provenance(result)
            if result.status not in (ExecutionStatus.SUCCESS, ExecutionStatus.COMPLETED):
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary=f"JADX execution {result.status.value.lower()} (exit code {result.exit_code}).",
                    error=result.status.value,
                    raw_data={"execution": provenance, "stderr": result.stderr[-2000:]},
                )

            # Count generated Java files
            sources_dir = sandbox_path(context, out_rel) / "sources"
            java_files = list(sources_dir.rglob("*.java")) if sources_dir.exists() else []

            produced_artifacts: List[ProducedArtifact] = []
            if java_files:
                produced_artifacts.append(
                    ProducedArtifact(
                        artifact_type=ArtifactType.DECOMPILED_JAVA,
                        storage_uri=str(sources_dir),
                        size_bytes=sum(f.stat().st_size for f in java_files),
                        producer=self.name,
                        producer_version=self.version,
                        metadata={"execution_id": result.execution_id, "parent_artifact_id": tool_input.artifact_id},
                    )
                )

            summary = f"JADX decompiled {len(java_files)} Java classes from {tool_input.artifact_id}"
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS if java_files else ToolExecutionStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                output_summary=summary,
                produced_artifacts=produced_artifacts,
                raw_data={
                    "execution": provenance,
                    "classes_count": len(java_files),
                    "sources_path": str(sources_dir),
                    "sample_classes": [str(p.relative_to(sources_dir)) for p in java_files[:10]],
                },
            )

        except Exception as e:
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                output_summary=f"JADX execution failed: {e}",
                error=str(e),

            # Normalize class format (e.g. com.example.Foo -> Lcom/example/Foo;)
            if not target_cls.startswith("L") and not target_cls.endswith(";"):
                norm_cls = f"L{target_cls.replace('.', '/')};"
            else:
                norm_cls = target_cls

            method_pattern = tool_input.target_method or ".*"

            for m in dx.find_methods(classname=re_escape_class(norm_cls), methodname=method_pattern):
                # Callers (who calls this method)
                if tool_input.direction in ("callers", "both"):
                    for caller_cls, call, offset in m.get_xref_from():
                        callers.append({
                            "caller_class": str(caller_cls.name),
                            "caller_method": str(call.name),
                            "offset": str(offset),
                        })

                # Callees (who this method calls)
                if tool_input.direction in ("callees", "both"):
                    for callee_cls, call, offset in m.get_xref_to():
                        callees.append({
                            "callee_class": str(callee_cls.name),
                            "callee_method": str(call.name),
                            "offset": str(offset),
                        })

[TOOL RESULT | SUCCESS]
"""
Unit and Contract tests for KAVACH AI 2.0 Content-Addressed ArtifactStore.
Validates:
1. Cryptographic content addressability (SHA-256 CAS)
2. Bit-for-bit immutability and tamper detection (ArtifactIntegrityError)
3. Opaque handle syntax and path traversal rejection
4. Run-scoped access isolation (ArtifactAccessViolation)
5. Complete chain-of-custody lineage (parents, execution ID, tool call ID)
6. Safe sandbox staging without CAS repository mutation
"""

import hashlib
import os
import stat
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

from storage.models import (
    StoredArtifact,
    ArtifactIntegrityError,
    ArtifactNotFoundError,
    ArtifactAccessViolation,
)
from storage.backend import FilesystemStorageBackend, MemoryStorageBackend
from storage.store import ArtifactStore


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_memory_storage_backend():
    backend = MemoryStorageBackend()
    data = b"dex_bytes_payload_test"
    sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

    store_key = await backend.write(sha, data)
    assert store_key.startswith("mem/")
    assert await backend.exists(store_key)

    read_bytes = await backend.read(store_key, expected_sha256=sha)
    assert read_bytes == data

    # Wrong expected hash fails
    with pytest.raises(ArtifactIntegrityError):
        await backend.read(store_key, expected_sha256="0" * 64)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_filesystem_storage_backend_tamper_detection(tmp_path: Path):
    backend = FilesystemStorageBackend(tmp_path / "cas_root")
    data = b"secure_banking_malware_sample_payload"
    sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

    store_key = await backend.write(sha, data)
    assert store_key == f"cas/{sha[:2]}/{sha}"
    assert await backend.exists(store_key)

    # Verify read
    assert await backend.read(store_key, expected_sha256=sha) == data

    # File on disk is read-only
    disk_path = backend.root_dir / store_key
    file_stat = disk_path.stat()
    assert not (file_stat.st_mode & stat.S_IWUSR), "CAS blob must be read-only"

    # Simulate deliberate external disk corruption/tamper
    disk_path.chmod(0o644)
    with open(disk_path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(b"corrupted_tampered_payload")
    disk_path.chmod(0o444)

    # Reading with integrity check must fail
    with pytest.raises(ArtifactIntegrityError):
        await backend.read(store_key, expected_sha256=sha)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_filesystem_backend_path_traversal_rejection(tmp_path: Path):
    backend = FilesystemStorageBackend(tmp_path / "cas_root")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Path traversal"):
        await backend.read("../outside_key")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_artifact_store_put_and_handle_resolution(tmp_path: Path):
    store = ArtifactStore.create_filesystem_store(tmp_path / "art_store")
    payload = b"class Lcom/bank/Trojan; -> stealth()"
    run_id = "run_audit_001"

    art = await store.put_bytes(
        run_id=run_id,
        content=payload,
        artifact_type="CARVED_DEX",
        producer="payload_decoder",
        producer_version="2.1.0",
        parent_artifact_ids=["art_original_apk"],
        produced_by_execution_id="exec_broker_999",
        produced_by_tool_call_id="call_decode_42",
        mime_type="application/octet-stream",
        metadata={"entropy": 7.84},
    )

    assert art.artifact_id.startswith("art_")
    assert art.handle == f"artifact://{art.artifact_id}"
    assert art.sha256 == hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
    assert art.parent_artifact_ids == ["art_original_apk"]
    assert art.produced_by_execution_id == "exec_broker_999"
    assert art.produced_by_tool_call_id == "call_decode_42"

    # Resolve handle with prefix
    resolved_by_handle = await store.resolve_handle(art.handle, run_id=run_id)
    assert resolved_by_handle.artifact_id == art.artifact_id

    # Resolve handle without prefix
    resolved_by_id = await store.resolve_handle(art.artifact_id, run_id=run_id)
    assert resolved_by_id.artifact_id == art.artifact_id

    # Fetch content
    retrieved = await store.get_content(art.artifact_id, run_id=run_id)
    assert retrieved == payload
    assert await store.verify_integrity(art.artifact_id) is True


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_artifact_store_run_scoping_isolation():
    store = ArtifactStore.create_memory_store()
    art_a = await store.put_bytes(
        run_id="run_A",
        content=b"confidential_evidence_run_A",
        artifact_type="REPORT",
        producer="reporter",
    )

    # Allowed in run_A
    assert (await store.get_artifact(art_a.artifact_id, run_id="run_A")).artifact_id == art_a.artifact_id

    # Cross-run access from run_B must be strictly rejected
    with pytest.raises(ArtifactAccessViolation, match="Access denied"):
        await store.get_artifact(art_a.artifact_id, run_id="run_B")

    with pytest.raises(ArtifactAccessViolation, match="Access denied"):
        await store.get_content(art_a.artifact_id, run_id="run_B")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_artifact_store_handle_validation():
    store = ArtifactStore.create_memory_store()

    invalid_handles = [
        "artifact://../escape",
        "artifact://foo/bar",
        "artifact://",
        "artifact://.",
        "artifact://..",
        "artifact://path\\escape",
        "/etc/passwd",
        "../test.apk",
    ]

    for inv in invalid_handles:
        with pytest.raises(ArtifactNotFoundError):
            await store.resolve_handle(inv)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_artifact_store_staging(tmp_path: Path):
    cas_dir = tmp_path / "cas"
    sandbox_dir = tmp_path / "sandbox_workspace"
    store = ArtifactStore.create_filesystem_store(cas_dir)

    apk_bytes = b"PK\x03\x04fake_apk_archive_stream"
    art = await store.put_bytes(
        run_id="run_staging_test",
        content=apk_bytes,
        artifact_type="REPAIRED_APK",
        producer="container_repair",
    )

    staged_path = await store.stage_artifact(
        artifact_id=art.artifact_id,
        dest_dir=sandbox_dir,
        run_id="run_staging_test",
        filename="target_repaired.apk",
        read_only=True,
    )

    assert staged_path.exists()
    assert staged_path.name == "target_repaired.apk"
    assert staged_path.read_bytes() == apk_bytes

    # Staging must not mutate or move the source in CAS
    cas_source = cas_dir / art.store_key
    assert cas_source.exists()
    assert cas_source.read_bytes() == apk_bytes


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_artifact_store_put_file(tmp_path: Path):
    store = ArtifactStore.create_memory_store()
    source_file = tmp_path / "extracted.dex"
    source_content = b"dex\n035\x00header_and_opcodes"
    source_file.write_bytes(source_content)

    art = await store.put_file(
        run_id="run_file_test",
        source_path=source_file,
        artifact_type="DEX",
        producer="dex_extractor",
    )

    assert art.size_bytes == len(source_content)
    assert art.sha256 == hashlib.sha256(source_content).hexdigest()
    assert await store.get_content(art.artifact_id) == source_content
    async def save_artifact(
        cls, artifact: StoredArtifact, stage_execution_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> ArtifactModel:
        """Persist or update an artifact descriptor in canonical storage."""
        async with get_session() as session:
            stmt = select(ArtifactModel).where(ArtifactModel.id == artifact.artifact_id)
            res = await session.execute(stmt)
            existing = res.scalar_one_or_none()

            created_dt = (
                artifact.created_at
                if isinstance(artifact.created_at, datetime)
                else datetime.fromisoformat(str(artifact.created_at))
            )
            meta_json = artifact.to_metadata_dict()

            if existing:
                existing.artifact_type = artifact.artifact_type
                existing.sha256 = artifact.sha256
                existing.size_bytes = artifact.size_bytes
                existing.mime_type = artifact.mime_type
                existing.storage_uri = artifact.storage_uri
                existing.producer = artifact.producer
                existing.producer_version = artifact.producer_version
                existing.metadata_json = meta_json
                return existing

            model = ArtifactModel(
                id=artifact.artifact_id,
                run_id=artifact.run_id,
                stage_execution_id=stage_execution_id,
                artifact_type=artifact.artifact_type,
                sha256=artifact.sha256,
                size_bytes=artifact.size_bytes,
                mime_type=artifact.mime_type,
                storage_uri=artifact.storage_uri,
                producer=artifact.producer,
                producer_version=artifact.producer_version,
                created_at=created_dt,
                metadata_json=meta_json,
            )
            session.add(model)
            await session.flush()
            return model

    @classmethod
    async def get_artifact_model(cls, artifact_id: str) -> Optional[ArtifactModel]:
        """Fetch an artifact model by ID."""
        async with get_session() as session:
            stmt = select(ArtifactModel).where(ArtifactModel.id == artifact_id)
            res = await session.execute(stmt)
            return res.scalar_one_or_none()

    @classmethod
    async def list_artifact_models(cls, run_id: str) -> List[ArtifactModel]:
        """List all artifact models belonging to an analysis run."""
        async with get_session() as session:
            stmt = select(ArtifactModel).where(ArtifactModel.run_id == run_id)
            res = await session.execute(stmt)
            return list(res.scalars().all())

"""
Database engine and session management for KAVACH AI 2.0.

from __future__ import annotations

    async_sessionmaker,
    AsyncSession,
    AsyncEngine,
)

from pathlib import Path

# Ensure backend .env is loaded

if _env_path.exists():
    load_dotenv(_env_path)

_engine: AsyncEngine | None = None
_session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession] | None = None


def _postgres_password() -> str:
    """Read the password from POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE (Docker/Compose secret) or POSTGRES_PASSWORD.

    There is no default password: a missing credential fails closed.
    """
    password_file = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE")
    if password_file:
        return Path(password_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    password = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD")
    if password:
        return password.strip()
    raise RuntimeError("PostgreSQL password not configured: set POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE or POSTGRES_PASSWORD")


def get_postgres_url() -> str:
    """Build the psycopg 3 PostgreSQL URL (used by both the async engine and Alembic)."""
    host = os.getenv("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost").strip()
    port = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432").strip()
    db_name = os.getenv("POSTGRES_DB", "kavach_db").strip()
    user = os.getenv("POSTGRES_USER", "kavach_user").strip()
    password = quote_plus(_postgres_password())
    return f"postgresql+psycopg://{quote_plus(user)}:{password}@{host}:{port}/{db_name}"


# Backwards-compatible name; psycopg 3 uses the same URL for sync and async engines.
get_postgres_async_url = get_postgres_url


def get_async_engine() -> AsyncEngine:
    """Get or initialize singleton AsyncEngine with bounded local pool."""
    global _engine
    if _engine is None:
        url = get_postgres_async_url()
        _engine = create_async_engine(
            url,
            pool_size=5,
            max_overflow=10,
            pool_timeout=30,
            pool_recycle=1800,
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            echo=False,
        )
    return _engine


def get_async_session_factory() -> async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]:
    """Get or initialize async session factory."""

    def evaluate_finalization(
        self,
        investigation: Investigation,
        known_artifacts: Dict[str, str],
        inspected_artifacts: Set[str],
        available_tool_names: Set[str],
    ) -> FrontierEvaluation:
        """
        Evaluate whether the model's request to conclude the investigation is permissible.
        Rule: Actionable frontier blocks finalization. Remaining budget alone does not.
        """
        reason_codes: List[ReasonCode] = []
        actionable_items: List[str] = []
        unresolved_hyp_ids: List[str] = []
        uninspected_art_ids: List[str] = []

        # 1. Check unresolved hypotheses
        for h in investigation.hypotheses:
            if h.epistemic_status in (EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED, EpistemicStatus.IN_PROGRESS):
                # Is there a viable tool in available tools to test this hypothesis?
                viable = self._is_hypothesis_testable(h, available_tool_names)
                if viable:
                    unresolved_hyp_ids.append(h.id)
                    actionable_items.append(
                        f"Unresolved hypothesis [{h.claim_type}] on {h.target_identifier}: '{h.claim_summary}' has viable testing tools available."
                    )
                else:
                    logger.debug(f"Hypothesis {h.id} ({h.claim_type}) has no viable available tools.")

        # 2. Check uninspected derived artifacts
        original_names = {"original_apk"}

        for art_key in known_artifacts:
            if art_key in original_names or art_key in inspected_artifacts:
                continue
            # Viable analysis path exists if workbench tools (python_exec, shell_exec, inspect_artifact) exist
            if any(t in available_tool_names for t in ("inspect_artifact", "read_file", "python_exec", "shell_exec")):
                uninspected_art_ids.append(art_key)
                actionable_items.append(
                    f"Derived artifact 'artifact://{art_key}' was produced but has not yet been inspected."
                )

        # Decision synthesis
        if unresolved_hyp_ids or uninspected_art_ids:
            if unresolved_hyp_ids:
                reason_codes.append(ReasonCode.FINALIZE_REJECTED_UNRESOLVED_HYPOTHESIS)
            if uninspected_art_ids:
                reason_codes.append(ReasonCode.FINALIZE_REJECTED_UNINSPECTED_ARTIFACT)

            guidance = (
                "Finalization rejected by Frontier Gate because an actionable investigation frontier remains:\n"
                + "\n".join(f"- {item}" for item in actionable_items)
                + "\nYou must inspect these recovered artifacts or test the remaining hypotheses before concluding."
            )

class ReportEngine:
    @staticmethod
    def generate(investigation: Investigation, controller: Any) -> ForensicReport:
        evidence_list = getattr(controller, "last_evidence", [])
        known_artifacts = getattr(controller, "last_artifacts", {})

        # 1. Key Findings from hypotheses and verified high-severity evidence
        key_findings: List[Finding] = []
        for h in investigation.hypotheses:
            if h.epistemic_status == EpistemicStatus.CONFIRMED:
                status = FindingStatus.VERIFIED
            elif h.epistemic_status == EpistemicStatus.REFUTED:
                status = FindingStatus.REFUTED
            else:
                status = FindingStatus.UNVERIFIED

            key_findings.append(Finding(
                finding_id=h.id,
                title=h.claim_type,
                status=status,
                description=h.claim_summary,
                evidence_ids=h.supporting_evidence_ids,
                artifact_ids=[],
                tools_used=[],
                confidence=0.95 if status == FindingStatus.VERIFIED else 0.5,
                mitre_technique_ids=[]
            ))

            unresolved_regions=[],
            investigation_trace=trace,
            evidence_ids=[ev.id for ev in evidence_list],
            artifact_ids=list(known_artifacts.values()),
        )
        return report

@asynccontextmanager
async def get_session() -> AsyncGenerator[AsyncSession, None]:
    """
    Context manager providing an isolated AsyncSession per task/transaction.
    Never share sessions across concurrent tasks.
    """
    factory = get_async_session_factory()
    async with factory() as session:
        try:
            yield session
            await session.commit()
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
            raise


async def dispose_async_engine() -> None:
    """Explicit shutdown lifecycle for async engine."""
    global _engine, _session_factory
    if _engine is not None:
        await _engine.dispose()
        _engine = None
        _session_factory = None

        self,
        apk_path: str,
        work_dir: str,
        run_id: str = "reconstruct",
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executes autonomous multi-pass reconstruction on target sample:
        Pass 1: Container repair & header normalization
        Pass 2: High-entropy & magic payload discovery
        Pass 3: Carving & static decoding
        Returns generated lineage nodes, derived artifacts, and new evidence.
        """
        os.makedirs(work_dir, exist_ok=True)
        evidence_accum: List[Evidence] = []
        new_artifacts: List[ProducedArtifact] = []

        import hashlib
        root_bytes = open(apk_path, "rb").read(65536)
        hasher = hashlib.sha256()
        with open(apk_path, "rb") as f:
            while chunk := f.read(65536):
                hasher.update(chunk)
        root_hash = hasher.hexdigest()

        root_node = LineageNode(
            artifact_id=root_hash,
            parent_id=None,
            artifact_type=ArtifactType.ORIGINAL_APK,
            storage_path=apk_path,
            sha256=root_hash,
            size_bytes=os.path.getsize(apk_path),
            producer_tool="original_input",
            operation="input_mount",
            verified=True,
        )
        self.lineage_graph[root_hash] = root_node

        active_apk_path = apk_path
        active_parent_hash = root_hash

import pytest
import os
import json
from pathlib import Path
from agent.controller import InvestigationController
from intelligence.gateway import ModelGateway

from domain.investigation import InvestigationStatus
from reporting.report_engine import ReportEngine
from tools.registry import ToolRegistry
from tools.base import BaseTool, ToolFamily, ToolOutput, ToolExecutionStatus
from analyzers.results import ProducedArtifact
from analyzers.capabilities import ArtifactType
from domain.evidence import Evidence, EvidenceOrigin, EvidenceSeverity, EvidenceConfidence
from domain.investigation import Hypothesis, EpistemicStatus

@pytest.fixture
def mock_registry():
    return build_investigation_tool_registry()

@pytest.fixture
def mock_supervisor():
    return InvestigationManager(initial_turn_budget=3, max_extensions=1, extension_turns=1)

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_a_valid_tool_call(mock_registry, mock_supervisor, tmp_path):
    provider = MockReasoningProvider([
        ProviderResponse(tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="1", name="search_strings", arguments={"artifact_id": "artifact://original_apk", "pattern": "HTTP"})])
    ])
    controller = InvestigationController(ModelGateway(provider, MODEL_PROFILES["mock-dev-fast"]), mock_registry, mock_supervisor)
    inv = await controller.run_investigation("run1", "samp1", "test.apk", [], str(tmp_path))
    assert len(inv.steps) >= 1
    assert inv.steps[0].tool_name == "search_strings"

class _ScriptedTool(BaseTool):
    """Test double bound to a real tool schema; returns a scripted ToolOutput per call."""

    version = "test"
    category = "test"
    family = ToolFamily.RECONSTRUCTION
    description = "scripted"

    def __init__(self, name: str, outputs):
        self.name = name
        self._outputs = list(outputs)

    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
        return True

    async def execute(self, tool_input, context) -> ToolOutput:
        return self._outputs.pop(0)


def _derived(tmp_path: Path, sha: str) -> ProducedArtifact:
    path = tmp_path / f"derived_{sha}.bin"
    path.write_bytes(b"derived")
    return ProducedArtifact(
        id=f"art_{sha}", artifact_type=ArtifactType.ORIGINAL_APK, storage_uri=str(path),
        sha256=sha, size_bytes=7, producer="test", producer_version="1",
    )


def _verified_evidence(ev_type: str, sha: str) -> Evidence:
    return Evidence(
        run_id="r", origin=EvidenceOrigin.RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED, stage="reconstruction", analyzer="test",
        category="reconstruction", type=ev_type, title=ev_type, source="test", normalized_value=sha,
        severity=EvidenceSeverity.HIGH, confidence=EvidenceConfidence.HIGH,
    )


def _ok(tool: str, summary: str = "ok", artifacts=(), evidence=()) -> ToolOutput:
    return ToolOutput(tool_name=tool, status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS, duration_seconds=0.0,
                      output_summary=summary, produced_artifacts=list(artifacts), new_evidence=list(evidence))


def _hyp(claim: str) -> Hypothesis:
    return Hypothesis(investigation_id="inv", target_identifier="artifact://original_apk",
                      claim_type=claim, claim_summary=claim, epistemic_status=EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED)


def _controller(provider, tools, supervisor=None) -> InvestigationController:
    registry = ToolRegistry()
    for t in tools:
        registry.register(t)
    return InvestigationController(ModelGateway(provider, MODEL_PROFILES["mock-dev-fast"]), registry,
                                   supervisor or InvestigationManager())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_e_recovered_artifacts(tmp_path):
    """A derived artifact becomes an opaque handle for the model and a resolvable entry below the boundary."""
    art = _derived(tmp_path, "e1")
    provider = MockReasoningProvider([
        ProviderResponse(tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="1", name="decode_static_payload",
                                              arguments={"artifact_id": "artifact://original_apk", "algorithm": "base64"})]),
        ProviderResponse(content="Done"),
    ])
    controller = _controller(provider, [_ScriptedTool("decode_static_payload", [_ok("decode_static_payload", artifacts=[art])])])
    await controller.run_investigation("run_e", "samp_e", str(tmp_path / "missing.apk"), [], str(tmp_path / "w"), max_turns=3)

    assert controller.last_artifacts["art_e1"] == art.storage_uri
    model_visible = json.dumps([c["messages"] for c in provider.call_history])
    assert "artifact://art_e1" in model_visible
    assert art.storage_uri not in model_visible

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_f_verified_hypothesis_promotes(tmp_path):
    art = _derived(tmp_path, "f1")
    ev = _verified_evidence("CONTAINER_EVASION_REPAIRED", "f1")
    hyp = _hyp("ZIP_CORRUPTION")
    provider = MockReasoningProvider([
        ProviderResponse(tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="1", name="repair_container", arguments={"artifact_id": "artifact://original_apk"})]),
        ProviderResponse(content="Done"),
    ])
    controller = _controller(provider, [_ScriptedTool("repair_container", [_ok("repair_container", artifacts=[art], evidence=[ev])])])
    await controller.run_investigation("run_f", "samp_f", str(tmp_path / "missing.apk"), [], str(tmp_path / "w"),
                                       initial_hypotheses=[hyp], max_turns=3)

    assert hyp.epistemic_status == EpistemicStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert hyp.promoted_evidence_id == ev.id


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_g_unverified_hypothesis_no_evidence(tmp_path):
    """Tool success without a bound, verified evidence item must not confirm the claim."""
    hyp = _hyp("HIDDEN_PAYLOAD")
    provider = MockReasoningProvider([
        ProviderResponse(tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="1", name="search_strings",
                                              arguments={"artifact_id": "artifact://original_apk", "pattern": "payload"})]),
        ProviderResponse(content="Done"),
    ])
    controller = _controller(provider, [_ScriptedTool("search_strings", [_ok("search_strings", "payload marker found")])])
    await controller.run_investigation("run_g", "samp_g", str(tmp_path / "missing.apk"), [], str(tmp_path / "w"),
                                       initial_hypotheses=[hyp], max_turns=3)

    assert hyp.epistemic_status == EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED
    assert hyp.promoted_evidence_id is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_h_replan_after_failure(tmp_path):
    """A failed call is reported to the model, which replans; the investigation continues."""
    failed = ToolOutput(tool_name="search_strings", status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED, duration_seconds=0.0,
                        output_summary="pattern engine unavailable", error="ENGINE_DOWN")
    provider = MockReasoningProvider([
        ProviderResponse(tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="1", name="search_strings",
                                              arguments={"artifact_id": "artifact://original_apk", "pattern": "x"})]),
        ProviderResponse(tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="2", name="inspect_container", arguments={"artifact_id": "artifact://original_apk"})]),
        ProviderResponse(content="Done"),
    ])
    controller = _controller(provider, [
        _ScriptedTool("search_strings", [failed]),
        _ScriptedTool("inspect_container", [_ok("inspect_container", "container ok")]),
    ])
    inv = await controller.run_investigation("run_h", "samp_h", str(tmp_path / "missing.apk"), [], str(tmp_path / "w"), max_turns=4)

    assert [s.tool_name for s in inv.steps if s.tool_name] == ["search_strings", "inspect_container"]
    assert inv.status == InvestigationStatus.COMPLETED
    second_turn_prompt = json.dumps(provider.call_history[1]["messages"])
    assert "FAILED" in second_turn_prompt and "pattern engine unavailable" in second_turn_prompt

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_frontier_gate_rejects_on_uninspected_derived_artifact(tmp_path: Path):
    """
    If a derived artifact exists in known_artifacts that has not been inspected,
    the Frontier Gate must reject finalization.
    """
    supervisor = InvestigationManager()
    inv = Investigation(run_id="run_art", sample_id="samp_art")
    known = {
        "original_apk": "/path/to/original.apk",
        "recovered_classes.dex": "forensic://recovered_classes.dex",
    }
    inspected = set()  # recovered_classes.dex not yet inspected!
    tools = {"inspect_artifact", "python_exec", "read_file"}

    eval_res: FrontierEvaluation = supervisor.evaluate_finalization(
        investigation=inv,
        known_artifacts=known,
        inspected_artifacts=inspected,
        available_tool_names=tools,
    )

    assert eval_res.decision == "REJECT"
    assert ReasonCode.FINALIZE_REJECTED_UNINSPECTED_ARTIFACT in eval_res.reason_codes
    assert "recovered_classes.dex" in eval_res.uninspected_artifacts[0]

    # Now simulate inspecting the artifact
    inspected.add("recovered_classes.dex")
    eval_res2 = supervisor.evaluate_finalization(
        investigation=inv,
        known_artifacts=known,
        inspected_artifacts=inspected,
        available_tool_names=tools,
    )
    assert eval_res2.decision == "ALLOW"
    assert ReasonCode.FINALIZE_ALLOWED_FRONTIER_EXHAUSTED in eval_res2.reason_codes


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_frontier_gate_allows_with_limitations_when_no_tools_exist(tmp_path: Path):
    """
    If an open hypothesis exists but no available tool can test it,
    finalization is allowed with limitations.
    """
    supervisor = InvestigationManager()
    inv = Investigation(run_id="run_lim", sample_id="samp_lim")
    inv.hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            investigation_id=inv.id,
            target_identifier="native_lib.so",
            claim_type="ARM64_CUSTOM_VIRTUALIZATION",
            claim_summary="Unknown proprietary VM bytecode",
            epistemic_status=EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED,
        )
    )
    known = {"original_apk": "/path/to/original.apk"}
    inspected = set()
    tools = set()  # No tools available to address custom VM

    eval_res = supervisor.evaluate_finalization(
        investigation=inv,
        known_artifacts=known,
        inspected_artifacts=inspected,
        available_tool_names=tools,
    )

    assert eval_res.decision == "ALLOW_WITH_LIMITATIONS"
    assert ReasonCode.FINALIZE_WITH_LIMITATIONS_NO_VIABLE_PATH in eval_res.reason_codes

def _verified_repair_evidence(repaired_sha256: str) -> Evidence:
    """Deterministic repair evidence bound to the derived artifact hash (required to confirm container claims)."""
    return Evidence(
        run_id="test",
        origin=EvidenceOrigin.RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED,
        stage="reconstruction",
        analyzer="repair_container",
        analyzer_version="1.0.0",
        category="reconstruction",
        type="CONTAINER_EVASION_REPAIRED",
        title="Container Evasion Repaired",
        description="Repaired header anomaly",
        severity=EvidenceSeverity.HIGH,
        confidence=EvidenceConfidence.HIGH,
        source="test.apk",
        normalized_value=repaired_sha256,
    )

        assert all(info.flag_bits & 0x1 for info in zf.infolist())

        {"tool": "repair_container", "artifact_id": "artifact://sample", "operation": "strip_encryption_flags"},

    assert res.new_evidence and res.new_evidence[0].normalized_value == repaired.sha256
    with zipfile.ZipFile(repaired.storage_uri) as zf:
        assert zf.testzip() is None
        assert not any(info.flag_bits & 0x1 for info in zf.infolist())


# ============================================================================
# Phase 0.6 Invariants: Schema Honesty, Broker Provenance, Aliasing, Authorization

def test_tool_schema_honesty_run_jadx(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """Schema honesty: tool schemas must only advertise parameters actually supported by implementation."""
    tool = registry.get_tool("run_jadx")
    assert tool is not None
    schema = registry._get_schema_for_tool(tool)
    props = schema["function"]["parameters"]["properties"]

    # Must advertise only implemented, broker-supported parameters
    assert "artifact_id" in props
    assert "deobfuscate" in props

    # Must NOT advertise unsupported or unhonored parameters
    assert "class_filter" not in props, "run_jadx must not advertise unsupported class_filter"
    assert "output_format" not in props, "run_jadx must not advertise unhonored output_format"


def test_registry_level_external_process_broker_provenance_invariant(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """
    Every tool in the investigation registry is strictly classified.
    Every EXTERNAL_PROCESS tool must be broker-mediated and its internal provenance
    must expose complete execution records.
    """
    tools = {name: registry.get_tool(name) for name in registry.list_tools()}
    expected_external = {"run_jadx", "inspect_elf_symbols", "python_exec", "shell_exec"}

    for name, tool in tools.items():
        assert tool is not None
        assert hasattr(tool, "execution_mode"), f"Tool {name} missing execution_mode"
        if name in expected_external:
            assert tool.execution_mode == ToolExecutionMode.EXTERNAL_PROCESS, (
                f"Tool {name} launches out-of-process executable and must be classified EXTERNAL_PROCESS"
            )
        else:
            assert tool.execution_mode == ToolExecutionMode.IN_PROCESS, (
                f"Tool {name} is in-process and must be classified IN_PROCESS"
            )


def test_artifact_handle_aliasing_same_basename(tmp_path: Path):
    """Artifact identity must be opaque and independent of filename or basename."""
    dir_a = tmp_path / "run_alpha"
    dir_b = tmp_path / "run_beta"
    dir_a.mkdir()
    dir_b.mkdir()

    file_a = dir_a / "classes.dex"
    file_b = dir_b / "classes.dex"
    file_a.write_bytes(b"dex_alpha_content_12345")
    file_b.write_bytes(b"dex_beta_content_67890")

    context = {
        "known_artifacts": {
            "art_alpha_1": str(file_a),
            "art_beta_2": str(file_b),
        }
    }

    # Handles are distinct despite identical basenames
    handle_a = "artifact://art_alpha_1"
    handle_b = "artifact://art_beta_2"
    assert handle_a != handle_b

    # Resolving each handle resolves strictly to its own file
    resolved_a = Path(resolve_artifact_handle(context, handle_a))
    resolved_b = Path(resolve_artifact_handle(context, handle_b))

    assert resolved_a == file_a
    assert resolved_b == file_b
    assert resolved_a.read_bytes() == b"dex_alpha_content_12345"
    assert resolved_b.read_bytes() == b"dex_beta_content_67890"

    # Resolving by basename alone is strictly rejected
    with pytest.raises(ArtifactHandleError):
        resolve_artifact_handle(context, "classes.dex")

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_artifact_persistence_lifecycle():
    from storage.models import StoredArtifact

    run_id = f"run_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    sample_id = f"samp_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
    now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    sample = Sample(
        id=sample_id,
        sha256=f"hash_{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
        file_size_bytes=1024,
        file_name="artifact_test.apk",
        storage_path="/tmp/test.apk",
        ingested_at=now_utc,
    )
    await PersistenceService.create_sample(sample)

    run = AnalysisRun(
        id=run_id,
        sample_id=sample_id,
        profile=AnalysisProfile.STANDARD_STATIC,
        started_at=now_utc,
    )
    await PersistenceService.create_analysis_run(run)

    art = StoredArtifact(
        artifact_id=f"art_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
        run_id=run_id,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        size_bytes=512,
        mime_type="application/octet-stream",
        artifact_type="CARVED_DEX",
        storage_uri="cas://" + "a" * 64,
        store_key=f"cas/aa/{'a' * 64}",
        parent_artifact_ids=["art_parent_001"],
        produced_by_execution_id="exec_999",
        produced_by_tool_call_id="call_111",
        producer="test_producer",
        producer_version="2.0.0",
        metadata={"entropy": 7.5},
    )

    db_art = await PersistenceService.save_artifact(art)
    assert db_art.id == art.artifact_id
    assert db_art.sha256 == "a" * 64
    assert db_art.metadata_json["parent_artifact_ids"] == ["art_parent_001"]
    assert db_art.metadata_json["produced_by_execution_id"] == "exec_999"

    fetched = await PersistenceService.get_artifact_model(art.artifact_id)
    assert fetched is not None
    assert fetched.id == art.artifact_id

    run_artifacts = await PersistenceService.list_artifact_models(run_id)
    assert len(run_artifacts) == 1
    assert run_artifacts[0].id == art.artifact_id

"""
Evidence and Observation Domain Entity for KAVACH AI 2.0.
Defines canonical evidence, origin taxonomy, severities, and in-memory aggregation store.
Enforces run_id as primary identifier while maintaining backward-compatible scan_id alias.
Uses timezone-aware UTC datetime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class EvidenceOrigin(str, Enum):
    STATIC = "STATIC"
    DYNAMIC = "DYNAMIC"
    NETWORK = "NETWORK"
    ML_INFERENCE = "ML_INFERENCE"
    AI_INTERPRETATION = "AI_INTERPRETATION"
    RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED = "RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED"


class EvidenceSeverity(str, Enum):
    CRITICAL = "CRITICAL"
    HIGH = "HIGH"
    MEDIUM = "MEDIUM"
    LOW = "LOW"
    INFO = "INFO"


class EvidenceConfidence(str, Enum):
    HIGH = "HIGH"
    MEDIUM = "MEDIUM"
    LOW = "LOW"


class EvidenceLocation(BaseModel):
    file_path: Optional[str] = None
    class_name: Optional[str] = None
    method_name: Optional[str] = None
    line_number: Optional[int] = None
    offset: Optional[int] = None
    manifest_element: Optional[str] = None


class Evidence(BaseModel):
    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    run_id: str = ""
    scan_id: str = ""  # Backward compatibility alias
    origin: EvidenceOrigin
    stage: str
    analyzer: str
    analyzer_version: str = "1.0.0"
    category: str
    type: str
    title: str
    description: Optional[str] = None
    severity: EvidenceSeverity = EvidenceSeverity.MEDIUM
    confidence: EvidenceConfidence = EvidenceConfidence.HIGH
    source: str
    artifact: Optional[str] = None
    location: Optional[EvidenceLocation] = None
    timestamp: datetime = Field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    raw_reference: Optional[Any] = None
    normalized_value: Optional[Any] = None
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
        if not self.run_id and self.scan_id:
            self.run_id = self.scan_id
        elif not self.scan_id and self.run_id:
            self.scan_id = self.run_id


# Canonical alias for 2.0 vocabulary
EvidenceItem = Evidence

altered\")\n        prev_hash = event.event_hash\n    if len(run_ids) > 1:\n        errors.append(f\"events from multiple runs in one chain: {sorted(run_ids)}\")\n    return class PolicyEngine:
    def __init__(self, policy_path: Optional[str] = None):
        self.policy: Dict[str, Any] = DEFAULT_POLICY
        if policy_path and os.path.exists(policy_path):
            try:
                with open(policy_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    loaded = yaml.safe_load(f)
                    if isinstance(loaded, dict):
                        self.policy = loaded
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            default_yaml_path = os.path.join(
                os.path.dirname(__file__), "policies", "v2_banking.yaml"
            )
            if os.path.exists(default_yaml_path):
                try:
                    with open(default_yaml_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        loaded = yaml.safe_load(f)
                        if isinstance(loaded, dict):
                            self.policy = loaded
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def evaluate(self, run_id: str, evidence_list: List[Evidence]) -> RiskAssessment:
        weights: Dict[str, float] = self.policy.get("weights", DEFAULT_POLICY["weights"])
        thresholds: Dict[str, int] = self.policy.get("thresholds", DEFAULT_POLICY["thresholds"])
        escalation_rules: List[Dict[str, Any]] = self.policy.get("escalation_rules", [])

        # Group evidence by category
        category_evidence: Dict[str, List[Evidence]] = {
            "signatures": [],
            "evasion": [],
            "permissions": [],
            "behavior": [],
            "crypto": [],
        }

        all_types: set[str] = set()
        all_categories: set[str] = set()

        for ev in evidence_list:
            all_types.add(ev.type)
            all_categories.add(ev.category)

            # Map domain evidence categories to scoring categories
            cat = ev.category.lower()
            if cat in ("signature", "signatures", "yara"):
                category_evidence["signatures"].append(ev)
            elif cat in ("evasion", "packer", "obfuscator", "protector"):
                category_evidence["evasion"].append(ev)
            elif cat in ("permission", "permissions"):
                category_evidence["permissions"].append(ev)
            elif cat in ("behavior", "behavioral", "quark"):
                category_evidence["behavior"].append(ev)
            elif cat in ("crypto", "cryptography"):
                category_evidence["crypto"].append(ev)
            else:
                # Default to behavior
                category_evidence["behavior"].append(ev)

        # Calculate sub-scores per category (0 to 100)
        sub_scores: Dict[str, float] = {}
        for cat, items in category_evidence.items():
            if not items:
                sub_scores[cat] = 0.0
                continue
            # Non-linear severity saturation: max_score * 0.7 + average_score * 0.3
            scores = [SEVERITY_SCORES.get(item.severity, 0.0) for item in items]
            max_s = max(scores)
            avg_s = sum(scores) / len(scores)
            sub_scores[cat] = round(min(100.0, (max_s * 0.7) + (avg_s * 0.3)), 1)

        # Compute initial weighted composite score
        composite_score: float = sum(
            sub_scores.get(cat, 0.0) * weights.get(cat, 0.0) for cat in weights
        )

        top_contributors: List[RiskContributor] = []

        # Check hard escalation rules
        escalated_threat_level: Optional[ThreatLevel] = None

        for rule in escalation_rules:
            rule_id = rule.get("id", "")
            rule_title = rule.get("title", rule_id)
            min_score = float(rule.get("min_score", 0))
            rule_threat = ThreatLevel(rule.get("threat_level", "HIGH"))

            triggered = False
            matched_evidence_ids: List[str] = []

            # 1. Required types check
            req_types = rule.get("required_evidence_types")
            if req_types:
                # If rule specifies a list where all or any match
                if rule_id == "RULE_ZIP_EVASION_ANOMALY":
                    # Any match is sufficient
                    for ev in evidence_list:
                        if ev.type in req_types:
                            triggered = True
                            matched_evidence_ids.append(ev.id)
                created_at=created_dt,
            )
            session.add(model)
            await session.flush()
            return model

    @classmethod
    async def finalize_analysis_run(
        cls, run_id: str, status: ExecutionState, error_message: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> None:
        """
        Finalize run execution lifecycle and record timing.
        """
        async with get_session() as session:
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            run_res = await session.execute(
                select(AnalysisRunModel).where(AnalysisRunModel.id == run_id)
            )
            run = run_res.scalar_one_or_none()
            if run:
                run.status = status.value if hasattr(status, "value") else str(status)
                run.completed_at = now
                if run.started_at:
                    duration = (now - run.started_at).total_seconds()
                    run.duration_seconds = max(0.0, duration)
                if error_message:
                    run.error_message = error_message

            logger.info(f"Finalized analysis run {run_id} with status {status}")

    @classmethod
    async def save_artifact(
        cls, artifact: StoredArtifact, stage_execution_id: Optional[str] = None

"""
AnalysisRun Domain Entity for KAVACH AI 2.0.
Tracks an orchestrated investigation lifecycle for an ingested APK sample.
Contains full configuration fingerprint for reproducibility and timezone-aware UTC datetime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from domain.execution_state import ExecutionState


class AnalysisProfile(str, Enum):
    STANDARD_STATIC = "STANDARD_STATIC"
    DEEP_STATIC = "DEEP_STATIC"
    FORENSIC_TRIAGE = "FORENSIC_TRIAGE"
    DYNAMIC_SANDBOX = "DYNAMIC_SANDBOX"
    FULL_SPECTRUM = "FULL_SPECTRUM"
    FAST = "FAST"
    STANDARD = "STANDARD"
    DEEP = "DEEP"


class AnalysisRun(BaseModel):
    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    sample_id: str
    status: ExecutionState = ExecutionState.QUEUED
    profile: AnalysisProfile = AnalysisProfile.STANDARD_STATIC
    started_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    completed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    duration_seconds: Optional[float] = None
    
    # Reproducibility & Configuration Fingerprint
    orchestrator_version: str = "2.0.0"
    kavach_git_commit: Optional[str] = None
    profile_version: str = "2.0.0"
    configuration_hash: Optional[str] = None
    risk_policy_version: str = "2.0.0"
    
    config_overrides: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    error_message: Optional[str] = None
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    def transition_to(self, new_state: ExecutionState, error: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Controlled state transition."""
        self.status = new_state
        if error:
            self.error_message = error
        if new_state in (ExecutionState.COMPLETED, ExecutionState.FAILED, ExecutionState.TIMEOUT, ExecutionState.SKIPPED):
            self.completed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            if self.started_at:
                self.duration_seconds = max(0.0, (self.completed_at - self.started_at).total_seconds())

[TOOL RESULT | SUCCESS]
"""
Claim-specific hypothesis adjudication.

A successful tool execution means only that the tool ran. A hypothesis is CONFIRMED only when
the tool output satisfies a deterministic evidence predicate registered for the hypothesis'
claim_type. Claims without a predicate, and outputs that do not satisfy one, leave the
hypothesis unresolved. A failed or inconclusive attempt never refutes a claim on its own.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable, Dict, FrozenSet, Optional

from domain.evidence import EvidenceOrigin
from domain.investigation import EpistemicStatus, Hypothesis
from tools.base import ToolExecutionStatus, ToolOutput


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Adjudication:
    new_status: EpistemicStatus
    evidence_id: str
    predicate: str


def _verified_artifact_evidence(
    output: ToolOutput,
    tool_name: str,
    required_tool: str,
    evidence_types: FrozenSet[str],
) -> Optional[str]:
    """Return the ID of verified evidence that is bound to an artifact this tool call produced.

    Requires: the expected tool, SUCCESS status, a produced artifact with a SHA-256, and
    RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED evidence of an accepted type whose normalized_value is that SHA-256.
    """
    if tool_name != required_tool or output.status != ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS:
        return None
    produced_hashes = {a.sha256 for a in output.produced_artifacts if a.sha256}
    for ev in output.new_evidence:
        if (
            ev.type in evidence_types
            and ev.origin == EvidenceOrigin.RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED
            and ev.normalized_value in produced_hashes
        ):
            return ev.id
    return None


def _container_repaired(tool_name: str, output: ToolOutput) -> Optional[str]:
    # repair_container reports SUCCESS only when the derived ZIP passes zipfile.testzip().
    return _verified_artifact_evidence(
        output, tool_name, "repair_container", frozenset({"CONTAINER_EVASION_REPAIRED"})
    )


def _payload_recovered(tool_name: str, output: ToolOutput) -> Optional[str]:
    # decode_static_payload emits these only for structurally verified DEX headers / ELF magic.
    return _verified_artifact_evidence(
        output, tool_name, "decode_static_payload", frozenset({"CARVED_DEX_RECOVERED", "CARVED_ELF_RECOVERED"})
    )


EvidencePredicate = Callable[[str, ToolOutput], Optional[str]]

CLAIM_PREDICATES: Dict[str, EvidencePredicate] = {
    "ZIP_CORRUPTION": _container_repaired,
    "CONTAINER_EVASION": _container_repaired,
    "ENCRYPTED_DEX": _payload_recovered,
    "HIDDEN_PAYLOAD": _payload_recovered,
}


def adjudicate(hypothesis: Hypothesis, tool_name: str, output: ToolOutput) -> Optional[Adjudication]:
    """Return a CONFIRMED adjudication if the output proves the claim, else None (unresolved)."""
    predicate = CLAIM_PREDICATES.get(hypothesis.claim_type)
    if predicate is None:
        return None
    evidence_id = predicate(tool_name, output)
    if evidence_id is None:
        return None
    return Adjudication(
        new_status=EpistemicStatus.CONFIRMED,
        evidence_id=evidence_id,
        predicate=predicate.__name__.lstrip("_"),
    )
