    async def execute(self, tool_input: FindPayloadCandidatesInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
        start_time = time.time()
        try:
            artifact_path = resolve_artifact_handle(context, tool_input.artifact_id)
        except ArtifactHandleError as e:
            return artifact_handle_rejection(self.name, start_time, e)

        if not os.path.exists(artifact_path):
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                output_summary=f"Artifact not found on disk: {tool_input.artifact_id}",
                error="ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND",
            )

        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        evidence_list: List[Evidence] = []
        p = Path(artifact_path)

        if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in (".apk", ".zip"):
            try:
                with zipfile.ZipFile(p, "r") as zf:
                    for info in zf.infolist():
                        if info.file_size < tool_input.min_size_bytes:
                            continue

                        # Check assets, res/raw, or root unusual files
                        is_target = (
                            info.filename.startswith("assets/")
                            or info.filename.startswith("res/raw/")
                            or not "/" in info.filename
                        )
                        if not is_target and not info.filename.endswith((".dex", ".bin", ".dat", ".enc", ".payload")):
                            continue

                        try:
                            sample_bytes = zf.read(info.filename)
                            entropy = calculate_shannon_entropy(sample_bytes)
                            magic = detect_magic_header(sample_bytes)

                            if entropy >= tool_input.min_entropy or magic != "UNKNOWN":
                                item = {
                                    "filename": info.filename,
                                    "size_bytes": info.file_size,
                                    "entropy": entropy,
                                    "detected_magic": magic,
                                    "compression_type": info.compress_type,
                                }
                                candidates.append(item)

                                # Create candidate evidence
                                sev = EvidenceSeverity.HIGH if (entropy > 7.5 or magic in ("DEX", "ELF")) else EvidenceSeverity.MEDIUM
                                evidence_list.append(
                                    Evidence(
                                        run_id=context.get("run_id", "manual"),
                                        origin=EvidenceOrigin.STATIC,
                                        stage="payload_discovery",
                                        analyzer=self.name,
                                        analyzer_version=self.version,
                                        category="payload",
                                        type="PAYLOAD_CANDIDATE_IDENTIFIED",
                                        title=f"Suspicious Payload Candidate: {info.filename}",
                                        description=f"Entry '{info.filename}' exhibits high entropy ({entropy}) and detected magic '{magic}' (Size: {info.file_size} bytes)",
                                        severity=sev,
                                        confidence=EvidenceConfidence.HIGH,
                                        source=info.filename,
                                        normalized_value=info.filename,
                                        metadata=item,
                                    )
                                )
                        except Exception:
                            pass
            except Exception as ze:
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary=f"Failed reading archive: {ze}",
                    error=str(ze),
                )

        # Sort by highest entropy first
        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: -x["entropy"])
        summary = f"Identified {len(candidates)} high-entropy / embedded payload candidate(s) in {p.name}"

        return ToolOutput(
            tool_name=self.name,
            status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS if candidates else ToolExecutionStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
            duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
            output_summary=summary,
            new_evidence=evidence_list,
            raw_data={
                "candidates_count": len(candidates),
                "candidates": candidates,
            },
        )

    async def execute(self, tool_input: DecodeStaticPayloadInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
        start_time = time.time()
        try:
            artifact_path = resolve_artifact_handle(context, tool_input.artifact_id)
        except ArtifactHandleError as e:
            return artifact_handle_rejection(self.name, start_time, e)

        if not os.path.exists(artifact_path):
            return ToolOutput(
                tool_name=self.name,
                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                output_summary=f"Artifact not found on disk: {tool_input.artifact_id}",
                error="ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND",
            )

        work_dir = Path(context.get("work_dir", "/tmp"))
        p = Path(artifact_path)
        raw_input = p.read_bytes()[tool_input.offset_bytes:]

        transformed = raw_input

        # 1. Apply transformation if specified
        if tool_input.algorithm == "base64":
            try:
                transformed = base64.b64decode(raw_input)
            except Exception as b64_err:
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary=f"Base64 decoding failed: {b64_err}",
                    error=str(b64_err),
                )

        elif tool_input.algorithm == "xor":
            if not tool_input.key_hex:
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary="XOR algorithm requires key_hex parameter",
                    error="MISSING_KEY",
                )
            key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(tool_input.key_hex)
            transformed = xor_transform(raw_input, key_bytes)

        elif tool_input.algorithm == "rc4":
            if not tool_input.key_hex:
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary="RC4 algorithm requires key_hex parameter",
                    error="MISSING_KEY",
                )
            key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(tool_input.key_hex)
            transformed = rc4_transform(raw_input, key_bytes)

        elif tool_input.algorithm in ("aes_cbc", "aes_ctr", "aes_ecb"):
            if not tool_input.key_hex:
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary=f"{tool_input.algorithm} requires key_hex parameter",
                    error="MISSING_KEY",
                )
            try:
                from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers import Cipher, algorithms, modes
                key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(tool_input.key_hex)
                iv_bytes = bytes.fromhex(tool_input.iv_hex) if tool_input.iv_hex else (b"\x00" * 16)

                if tool_input.algorithm == "aes_cbc":
                    cipher_mode = modes.CBC(iv_bytes)
                elif tool_input.algorithm == "aes_ctr":
                    cipher_mode = modes.CTR(iv_bytes)
                else:
                    cipher_mode = modes.ECB()

                cipher = Cipher(algorithms.AES(key_bytes), cipher_mode)
                decryptor = cipher.decryptor()
                transformed = decryptor.update(raw_input) + decryptor.finalize()
            except Exception as aes_err:
                return ToolOutput(
                    tool_name=self.name,
                    status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                    duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
                    output_summary=f"AES decryption error: {aes_err}",
                    error=str(aes_err),

    @classmethod
    def create_gateway(
        cls,
        model_id: Optional[str] = None,
        base_url: Optional[str] = None,
        override_provider: Optional[ReasoningProvider] = None,
    ) -> ModelGateway:
        """Construct a ModelGateway wired to the specified or active profile.

        Invariant: requested model identity == effective model identity.
        Implicit substitutions (such as legacy KAVACH_DEV_FAST) are forbidden.
        Mock models are strictly rejected in production environments.
        """
        mid = model_id or os.environ.get("KAVACH_MODEL_ID") or cls._active_model_id
        if mid not in MODEL_PROFILES:
            raise KeyError(f"Unknown model profile '{mid}'. Fail-closed: cannot create gateway.")
        profile = MODEL_PROFILES[mid]

        env = os.environ.get("KAVACH_ENV", "production").lower()
        if profile.provider_type == ProviderType.MOCK and env == "production":
            raise PermissionError(
                f"Model profile '{mid}' is a development/mock profile and is strictly forbidden when KAVACH_ENV=production."
            )

        if override_provider is not None:
            # Requested profile and effective provider must describe the same backend family.
            # Only an explicitly test-scoped environment may pair them freely.
            override_type = getattr(override_provider, "provider_type", None)
            if override_type != profile.provider_type and env != "test":
                raise PermissionError(
                    f"Provider override of type '{getattr(override_type, 'value', override_type)}' cannot serve "
                    f"profile '{mid}' ({profile.provider_type.value}) outside KAVACH_ENV=test."
                )
            return ModelGateway(provider=override_provider, profile=profile)

        if profile.provider_type == ProviderType.MOCK:
            provider = MockReasoningProvider()
        else:
            url = base_url or os.environ.get("KAVACH_LLAMA_BASE_URL") or "http://127.0.0.1:8092/v1"
            provider = LlamaCppReasoningProvider(base_url=url)

        return ModelGateway(provider=provider, profile=profile)

    async def chat_complete(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, str]],
        tools: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        tool_choice: Optional[str | Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        response_format: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        temperature: float = 0.1,
        max_tokens: int = 2048,
        reasoning_budget_tokens: Optional[int] = None,
        cache_prompt: bool = True,
    ) -> ProviderResponse:
        start_time = time.time()
        url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"

        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "max_tokens": max_tokens,
            "stream": False,
            "cache_prompt": cache_prompt,
        }

        if reasoning_budget_tokens is not None:
            payload["reasoning_budget_tokens"] = reasoning_budget_tokens
            if reasoning_budget_tokens == 0:
                payload["reasoning_effort"] = "none"

        if tools:
            payload["tools"] = tools
            if tool_choice:
                payload["tool_choice"] = tool_choice
            else:
                payload["tool_choice"] = "auto"

        if response_format:
            payload["response_format"] = response_format

        headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}",
        }

        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=self.timeout) as client:
            try:
                resp = await client.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
                resp.raise_for_status()
                data = resp.json()
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
                logger.error(f"Llama-server HTTP error {e.response.status_code}: {e.response.text}")
                raise RuntimeError(f"Llama-server returned error {e.response.status_code}: {e.response.text}") from e
            except httpx.RequestError as e:
                logger.error(f"Llama-server connection error: {e}")
                raise ConnectionError(f"Could not connect to llama-server at {url}: {e}") from e

        duration = round(time.time() - start_time, 3)

        choices = data.get("choices", [])
        timings = data.get("timings", {})
        usage = data.get("usage", {})
        if not choices:
            return ProviderResponse(
                content="",
                model=data.get("model", "unknown"),
                usage=usage,
                timings=timings,
                duration_seconds=duration,
                finish_reason="stop",
            )

        choice = choices[0]
        message = choice.get("message", {})
        raw_content = message.get("content")
        reasoning_content = message.get("reasoning_content")
        finish_reason = choice.get("finish_reason")

        parsed_tool_calls: List[ToolCall] = []

        # 1. Native OpenAI-style tool calls
        raw_tools = message.get("tool_calls", [])
        for idx, t in enumerate(raw_tools):
            fn = t.get("function", {})
            fn_name = fn.get("name", "")
            raw_args = fn.get("arguments", "{}")
            if isinstance(raw_args, str):
                try:
                    args_dict = json.loads(raw_args)
                except Exception:
                    args_dict = {"raw": raw_args}
            elif isinstance(raw_args, dict):
                args_dict = raw_args
            else:
                args_dict = {}

            parsed_tool_calls.append(
                ToolCall(
                    id=t.get("id", f"call_{idx}_{int(time.time())}"),
                    name=fn_name,
                    arguments=args_dict,
                )
            )

        # 2. Fallback heuristic: If no native tool calls but content contains XML or markdown JSON tool call

--- [Step 118 | ASSISTANT MESSAGE / PROGRESS] ---
I've stopped the golden demo fixture (Task 11). I'm not going to write a synthetic APK that imitates malware behaviour, and I deleted the partial generator file. Days 1 and part of 2 are done otherwise, but nothing in this session has been pushed, and the database tests have not run because Docker is stopped.

**Committed on `master` (3 commits, not pushed):**

1. **`9390574` ArtifactStore fixes (Task 2):**
   - Database write and lookup errors now fail the operation instead of being logged as success.
   - Listing a run's artifacts reads PostgreSQL, so artifacts survive a restart.
   - Artifact IDs are scoped to one run (new migration), and stored artifacts can't be changed.
   - The path check uses real path ancestry instead of a string prefix, and staging always copies instead of hardlinking.
   - JADX output folders are stored as deterministic ZIPs.
   - 10 new tests cover these, and the Postgres lifecycle test now includes a restart.
2. **`25c5833` ArtifactStore drives the agent (Task 1):** the controller no longer uses the `known_artifacts` host-path map. Tools resolve `artifact://` handles only through the store for the current run. Every artifact a tool produces is registered with its parent, execution ID and tool call ID.
3. **`123d610` Ledger and execution records (Tasks 3–4):**
   - Each run gets a hash-chained, append-only event log in Postgres; a database trigger refuses UPDATE and DELETE.
   - An independent checker catches tampering and gaps in the sequence.
   - The controller logs all 11 event types with causal links.
   - Every executed tool call produces an execution record. External tools use the sandbox's record; in-process parsers are recorded honestly with isolation `NONE`.
   - Evidence without a source artifact, execution and tool call is rejected before it reaches the ledger or can confirm a hypothesis.

**Not committed yet (in the working tree):**
- **InvestigationService:** `service/` holds the run records, static triage, the report built from the ledger, the service itself and the FastAPI routes (upload, status, events, live event stream, artifacts, verify, report).
- **Demo tool registry:** only the 6 demo tools are registered.
- **Offered-tools rule:** the controller now refuses any tool that wasn't offered to the model that turn, even if it's registered. I updated one existing test for this.
- **New dependencies:** I installed `fastapi`, `uvicorn` and `python-multipart` into `.venv`. They are not in `requirements.txt` yet.
- No tests exist for the service yet; it has not been run.

**Verification:**
- **Last full gate run (after the first two commits):** UNIT, CONTRACT, SECURITY and REPAIR passed.
  - INTEGRATION failed on 4 tests, all because nothing is listening on Postgres port 5432.
- **Ledger commit:** the ledger tests and affected suites passed (85 tests), but I did not rerun the full gate.
- **Never run:** the new Postgres tests for artifact restart and for the ledger trigger.
